# Research Analyses Timeline and Overview

This document provides a consolidated chronological timeline of research analyses across the 14 scanned trees, organized by technical domain. Analyses are ordered by dates stated in report titles, filenames, and evidence records.

> [!NOTE]
> In accordance with project governance rules, all wind tunnel facilities and commercial partners are referenced generically as 'the facility' or 'the vendor'.
